above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000552/B001","root_001008/B003","root_001040/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0d3361ed40c74f2bcefd","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"32:6:2\",\"root_001040\",[\"B002\"]],[\"32:6:5\",\"root_001008\",[\"B003\"]],[\"32:6:6\",\"root_000552\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Sonraki ayetlerdeki işaretler, erişilmesi güç görünmeyen bilgiye görünen dünyadaki ayırt edici izler üzerinden ulaşılabildiğini düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000822/B001","root_000822/B002","root_001040/B001","root_001117/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_16f73066d471eb122a7d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"32:6:2\",\"root_001040\",[\"B001\"]],[\"32:6:3\",\"root_001117\",[\"B001\"]],[\"32:6:4\",\"root_000822\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Görünmeyen ile görünenin birlikte bilinmesi, gizli başlangıçlardan gözlemlenen sonuçlara kadar bütün dönüşüm aşamalarını tek bir bilgi alanında toplar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000552/B001","root_001008/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_170e2b37baa4454b0996","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"32:6:5\",\"root_001008\",[\"B001\"]],[\"32:6:6\",\"root_000552\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Güç ile merhametin yan yana gelişi, zayıf maddeden hayat kurma ile ölümden sonra eksiksiz geri alma işlerini aynı kudretin tamamlayıcı yönleri yapar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000552/B001","root_001008/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2fcd1a1182907ac7d510","text":"3. **Might and mercy are complementary capacities.** `ع ز ز:B001` gives might, invulnerability, and honor; `ر ح م:B001` gives mercy and beneficence. The fragile origins and bestowed senses of 32:7–9 activate beneficence, while dissolution in 32:10 and the delegated taking and return of 32:11 activate irresistible control. **Inferred arrow:** beneficent formation + unconstrained recovery -> one continuous power over origin and return. The closing attributes read as functionally paired rather than affectively opposed.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001117/B002","root_001117/B009"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_30762dadb7fe8b687a32","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"32:6:3\",\"root_001117\",[\"B002\",\"B009\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Toprağa gömülüp görünmez olma itirazı, görünmeyenin bilgisi içinde kalan somut bir saklanma durumu olarak okunabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001117/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_78dfe9fd9bcb032c3d13","text":"2. **Burial is a concrete edge case of the unseen.** Exploratorily, `غ ي ب:B009` supplies hiding by burial, while `غ ي ب:B002` supplies a low place in which something disappears. Within this ayah’s forward window, 32:10 imagines bodies lost in the earth and 32:11 names death and return. The packet supplies disappearance in earth; **the inferred arrow is:** terrestrial concealment -> a physical instance within the knower’s unseen domain. The epithet anticipatorily answers the resurrection objection: becoming hidden in earth does not become hidden from this knowledge.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000822/B001","root_001117/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7ee4b5a229cec1e57bfd","text":"1. **Knowledge spans latent process and manifest result.** `غ ي ب:B001` is what is hidden from sight or human knowledge; `ش ه د:B001` is presence with direct witnessing, and `ع ل م:B001` is apprehension as a thing becomes clear. In 32:7–9, clay, extracted fluid, formation, spirit, hearing, sight, and hearts unfold from material origin to perceptible human capacities. **Inferred arrow:** access to both concealed antecedents and witnessed outcomes -> knowledge of the whole transformation. The pair *al-ghayb wa-l-shahāda* thus reads dynamically across stages of formation, not merely as two static territories.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_939a33703fd598a909a4","text":"The hidden recompense of 32:17 preserved the unseen side, while ruins in 32:26 and irrigated earth in 32:27 supplied visible signs on the witnessed side. Their pairing made **ع ل م B002**, knowledge through a distinguishing mark, much more active than it first appeared.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_da57477922df914119e0","text":"Later 32:11 supplied a concrete bridge between hidden and witnessed domains: what appears as death is a complete receipt (`و ف ي:B002`) executed by an assigned agent.
